# Persona Catalog

Reviewer personas are organized into a small core plus generic, cross-cutting, and stack-specific conditionals. You (the orchestrator) use this catalog to select only reviewers whose domain is present in the diff.

## Core and standards gate

Correctness is spawned on every multi-agent review. Project-standards is spawned only when Stage 3b (Discover project standards paths) finds at least one applicable standards file, or when standards discovery fails and the review must run the persona rather than silently skip it.

**Structured persona prompt assets:**

| Persona | Prompt asset | Focus |
|---------|-------|-------|
| `correctness` | `correctness-reviewer` | Logic errors, edge cases, state bugs, error propagation, intent compliance |
| `project-standards` | `project-standards-reviewer` | Compliance with the project's designated criteria files, cited rule by rule |

## Generic conditional

These reviewers are broadly applicable but not automatically useful. Spawn one only when the concrete surface it reviews is present in the diff.

| Persona / asset | Prompt asset | Select when diff touches... |
|---------|-------|-------|
| `testing` | `testing-reviewer` | Test files, test infrastructure, fixtures, mocks, or harness behavior; or meaningful runtime behavior changed without corresponding test work. Behavioral triggers include new or changed branches, state mutation, API/control-flow behavior, and error handling. Production-file presence alone and non-behavioral edits do not select it. |
| `maintainability` | `maintainability-reviewer` | Large or structural work: substantial refactors, new abstractions, file moves, coupling/type-boundary changes, or at least 200 executable changed lines. |
| `agent-native` | `agent-native-reviewer` | Agent-facing features or surfaces: skills, agents, prompts, commands, tools, MCP, or a product capability expected to be agent-accessible. |
| `learnings` | `learnings-researcher` | The change has institutional knowledge to be checked against: an existing `<root>/solutions/` corpus has a plausible path/title match for the changed modules or patterns (run a cheap search first; corpus existence alone does not select it), or, in local scope, the repo's CE config declares Compound Packs (Stage 1b `declared_packs`, semantics in `references/scope.md`). Declared packs select it without a pre-search; the persona matches every rule's `applies_when` itself. |

## Conditional (7 personas)

Spawn one of these when you identify relevant patterns in the diff. Read the full diff and reason about selection -- this is agent judgment, not keyword matching.

| Persona | Agent | Select when diff touches... |
|---------|-------|---------------------------|
| `security` | `security-reviewer` | Auth middleware, public endpoints, user input handling, permission checks (including feature-flag or entitlement gates that control whether functionality is reachable), secrets management |
| `performance` | `performance-reviewer` | Concrete performance-sensitive behavior: database/ORM query shape, algorithmic complexity, large loop-heavy transforms, batching/fan-out, or cache policy with material resource impact. Async/concurrent code or a cache data structure alone does not select it when correctness/reliability already own the changed semantics. |
| `api-contract` | `api-contract-reviewer` | An externally consumed boundary changes: route/request/response definitions, serializers, published event schemas, API versioning, or a public package signature with evidenced downstream callers. A new or changed exported symbol inside one module is insufficient by itself. |
| `data-migration` | `data-migration-reviewer` | Migration files, schema dumps (`db/schema.rb`, `structure.sql`), backfill scripts, data transformations — **not** model/query-only changes without migration artifacts |
| `reliability` | `reliability-reviewer` | Error handling, retry logic, circuit breakers, timeouts, background jobs, async handlers, health checks |
| `adversarial` | `adversarial-reviewer` fallback | >=50 changed code lines; auth/payments; persistence writes or event publication; retry/partial-failure or concurrency/ordering semantics; external APIs; or a silent-pass verification mechanism. The lens runs through the independent cross-model peer when a sanctioned peer starts. Spawn this in-process persona when the peer cannot start, when fold-in runs the did-not-run fallback, or when fold-in restores local after a failed same-route rate-limit retry. A started peer that produced a review artifact and the fallback are mutually exclusive. |
| `previous-comments` | `previous-comments-reviewer` | **PR-only AND comment-gated.** Reviewing a PR that has existing review comments or review threads from prior review rounds. Skip entirely when no PR metadata was gathered in Stage 1, OR when Stage 1's `hasPriorComments` flag is false (no `reviews` and no `comments` on the PR). |

## Stack-Specific Conditional (2 personas)

These reviewers cover specialized runtime behavior. Structural and maintainability concerns belong to the conditional `maintainability` persona — do not spawn extra stack reviewers for philosophy or convention-only passes.

| Persona | Agent | Select when diff touches... |
|---------|-------|---------------------------|
| `julik-frontend-races` | `julik-frontend-races-reviewer` | Stimulus/Turbo controllers, DOM event wiring, timers, async UI flows, animations, or frontend state transitions with race potential |
| `swift-ios` | `swift-ios-reviewer` | Swift files, SwiftUI views, UIKit controllers, `.entitlements`, `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy`, `.xcdatamodeld`, `Package.swift`, `Package.resolved`, storyboards, XIBs, or semantic build-setting / target-membership / code-signing changes in `.pbxproj` |

## CE Conditional Local Prompt Assets (migration-specific)

Use `deployment-verification-agent` when the migration-artifact condition (selection rule 5 below) applies **and** the change is risky (destructive DDL, backfills, NOT NULL without default, column renames/drops). Schema drift and migration safety belong to the `data-migration` persona — not a separate typed agent.

| Prompt asset | Focus |
|-------|-------|
| `deployment-verification-agent` | Go/No-Go deployment checklist with SQL verification queries and rollback procedures |

## Selection rules

1. **Always spawn correctness.** Spawn project-standards only for a non-empty applicable standards path list; skip it on a successful empty search, and spawn it when discovery is uncertain so an error never becomes a silent skip.
2. **For each generic conditional**, require its explicit surface. For `testing`, that surface is changed tests/harnesses or concrete meaningful runtime behavior with no corresponding test work; production-file presence alone is insufficient. Absence means skip, not "run just in case."
3. **For each cross-cutting conditional persona**, read the diff and decide whether its domain is relevant. This is a judgment call, not a keyword match.
4. **For each stack-specific conditional persona**, use file types and changed patterns as a starting point, then decide whether the diff actually introduces meaningful work for that reviewer. Do not spawn language-specific reviewers just because one config or generated file happens to match the extension.
5. **For `data-migration`**, spawn only when the diff includes migration or schema artifacts (`db/migrate/*`, `db/schema.rb`, `db/structure.sql`, Alembic/Flyway/Liquibase paths, or explicit backfill/data-transform scripts). Do **not** spawn for model-only or query-only changes without those files.
6. **For CE conditional prompt assets**, use `deployment-verification-agent` when the migration-artifact condition applies and the change is risky (see above).
7. **Announce the team** before spawning with a one-line justification per conditional reviewer selected.

# Selecting reviewers and binding the adversarial route

Read this at Stage 3 (Select reviewers), together with `references/persona-catalog.md`. It defines the layers reviewers are selected in, the search for project standards paths, the check that shrinks the reviewer list for a small low-risk diff, and the decision between the cross-model peer and the in-process adversarial reviewer.

## Reviewers

Reviewer personas are selected in layers. The persona catalog in `references/persona-catalog.md` (read it at Stage 3) has the full selection criteria and the condition for spawning each reviewer. Each selected reviewer is a generic subagent seeded with a local prompt file from `references/personas/`; do not dispatch standalone agents by type/name.

**Core (always-on):** `correctness-reviewer`.

**Standards conditional:** `project-standards-reviewer` runs only when Stage 3b finds at least one applicable standards file (Stage 3b is the project-standards path search below). When the search succeeds but finds nothing, skip this persona and say so in Coverage, because this persona is not allowed to invent standards beyond those files.

**Generic conditional:**

- `testing-reviewer` — test files, test infrastructure, mocks, fixtures, or harness behavior changed; or the diff changes meaningful runtime behavior without corresponding test work. Behavioral triggers include new or changed branches, state mutation, API/control-flow behavior, and error handling. Production-file presence alone and non-behavioral edits do not select it.
- `maintainability-reviewer` — a large or structural diff: substantial refactor, new abstractions, file moves, coupling/type-boundary changes, or at least 200 executable changed lines.
- `agent-native-reviewer` — an agent-facing feature or surface changed (skills, agents, prompts, tools, MCP, commands, or a product capability expected to be accessible to agents).
- `learnings-researcher` — there is institutional knowledge to check the change against: `<root>/solutions/` exists and a cheap path/title search finds a plausible match for the changed modules or patterns (the existence of a corpus alone is not enough), or, in local scope, the repo's CE config declares Compound Packs (Stage 1b `declared_packs`). Declared packs need no pre-search; the persona matches their rules itself.

**Cross-cutting conditional (per diff):**

- `security-reviewer` — auth, public endpoints, user input, permissions (including feature-flag or entitlement gates controlling reachability)
- `performance-reviewer` — DB queries, data transforms, caching, async
- `api-contract-reviewer` — routes, serializers, type signatures, versioning
- `data-migration-reviewer` — migration files / schema dumps / backfills (see the `data-migration` spawn gate in Stage 3)
- `reliability-reviewer` — error handling, retries, timeouts, background jobs
- `adversarial-reviewer` lens — >=50 changed code lines, or auth / payments / persistence writes / event publication / retry or concurrency semantics / external APIs, or a **silent-pass verification mechanism** regardless of size. Satisfy this lens with the independent cross-model adversarial pass when a peer job on the approved route starts successfully. Dispatch the in-process `adversarial-reviewer` when the peer cannot start, or when the fold-in step later finds the peer never ran, or gives up after a retry on the same route fails on a rate limit and restores the local reviewer; do not run both reviews on the same brief.
- `previous-comments-reviewer` — PR with existing review comments (PR-only, comment-gated)

**Stack-specific conditional (per diff):** `julik-frontend-races-reviewer` (Stimulus/Turbo, DOM events, async UI) and `swift-ios-reviewer` (Swift/SwiftUI/UIKit, entitlements, Core Data, `.pbxproj`).

**CE conditional (migration-specific):** local prompt asset `deployment-verification-agent` — deployment checklist + rollback when the migration gate applies and the change is risky.

## Review Scope

A full review always spawns correctness, adds project-standards when applicable files exist, then adds only the generic, cross-cutting, stack-specific, and CE conditionals justified by the diff. This file runs only on the full spine; it does not invent irrelevant domains. A Rails auth feature might add security, reliability, and adversarial while still skipping agent-native and learnings when those surfaces are absent.

## Language-Aware Conditionals

Select stack-specific reviewers only when the diff touches runtime behavior they specialize in (async UI races, iOS/Swift lifecycle), never mechanically from file extensions alone. The trigger is meaningful changed behavior in that stack's runtime domain. Structural quality (complexity deletion, 1k-line regressions, type-boundary leaks) belongs to the conditional `maintainability-reviewer`; do not spawn extra reviewers for language conventions, philosophy, or "strict bar" passes.

### Stage 3: Select reviewers

Read the diff and file list from Stage 1 and the helper JSON from Stage 1b. Correctness is automatic; project-standards is decided by the Stage 3b (Discover project standards paths) result. Read `references/persona-catalog.md` from this skill's directory now; it defines the spawn condition for every other reviewer. Select generic reviewers before domain reviewers: testing for changed test/harness surfaces, or when meaningful runtime behavior changed without corresponding test work; maintainability only for large or structural work; agent-native only for agent-facing work; and learnings when a cheap search finds plausible matches in an existing `<root>/solutions/` corpus or, in local scope, the repo declares Compound Packs (Stage 1b `declared_packs`). For the behavioral testing trigger, require concrete diff evidence such as new or changed branches, state mutation, API/control-flow behavior, or error handling. Do not select testing from production-file presence alone or for non-behavioral edits. For each remaining conditional, decide whether the diff warrants it. Diff-derived helper signals (`signals`, `test_files_changed`, `agent_surface`, `has_learnings_corpus`) are prompts to consider a persona, never automatic selection. `declared_packs` is a config fact the learnings selection condition reads, not a diff signal.

**File-type awareness for conditional selection:** Instruction-prose files (Markdown skill definitions, JSON schemas, config files) are product code but do not benefit from runtime-focused reviewers. The adversarial reviewer's techniques (race conditions, cascade failures, abuse cases) target executable code behavior. For diffs that only change instruction-prose files, skip adversarial unless the prose describes auth, payment, or data-mutation behavior, or the change is itself a silent-pass verification mechanism (next paragraph — a CI/CD workflow is a config file but still gets the adversarial lens). Count only executable code lines toward line-count thresholds.

Treat changed persistence writes, event publication, retry/partial-failure behavior, and concurrency or ordering semantics as concrete data-mutation/external-boundary triggers for `adversarial`; do not require a framework-specific database or HTTP keyword.

**Silent-pass verification mechanisms — select adversarial for the guard itself.** When the change *is* a verification mechanism — CI/CD gating logic, merge-blocking checks, build/deploy steps, coverage/lint gates, or test infrastructure/mocks that could mask production — its risk isn't blast radius, it's fidelity: it can go green while the real thing is red, so the exact "can this false-pass?" lens must run. Select `adversarial` (and therefore the Stage-4 cross-model pass) for such a change regardless of changed-line count and independent of the auth/data heuristics. The selection question: "If this mechanism is wrong, does it fail loudly or silently pass? A silent-pass guard gets the adversarial + cross-model lens regardless of size." Scope limit: this applies to the *mechanism* (gating/CI/build/deploy/harness changes), not to ordinary per-feature test assertions — a unit test asserting business logic is the `testing` reviewer's job, not adversarial's.

**`previous-comments` is PR-only AND comment-gated.** Only select this persona when both conditions hold:

1. Stage 1 gathered PR metadata (PR number or URL was provided as an argument, or `gh pr view` returned metadata for the current branch).
2. `hasPriorComments` from Stage 1 is true (the PR has at least one review submission or issue comment).

Skip it for standalone branch reviews with no associated PR, and skip it for PRs with no prior feedback yet -- there is nothing for the persona to verify, and a spawned subagent that returns empty findings still costs the full subagent startup overhead (persona spec, diff, schema, plus its own gh calls).

Stack-specific personas are additive when runtime behavior warrants them. A Hotwire UI change may warrant `julik-frontend-races`; a TypeScript boundary change may warrant `api-contract` only when the diff changes an externally consumed contract, not merely because it exports a symbol.

**`data-migration` spawn gate.** Select `data-migration-reviewer` only when the diff includes at least one migration or schema artifact: `db/migrate/*`, `db/schema.rb`, `db/structure.sql`, Alembic/Flyway/Liquibase migration paths, or explicit backfill/data-transform scripts (rake tasks, one-off data migration classes). **Do not spawn** for model-only changes, query-only refactors, serializers/controllers that reference columns without a migration or schema dump in the diff, or migration tests alone.

For `deployment-verification-agent`, use the same migration-artifact condition when the change is risky (destructive DDL, backfills, NOT NULL without default, column renames/drops).

### Stage 3b: Decide the project-standards dispatch

Stage 1c already paired each criteria file governing this change with the changed files it governs. Decide from that mapping whether the `project-standards` persona runs. When the instruction-file fallback supplied the criteria for any changed file, name it as the fallback in Coverage. **When uncertain, run the persona rather than skip it** — an error is never an empty result:

- One or more applicable paths: select `project-standards` and pass the mapping inside a `<standards-paths>` block in its Stage 4 context. The persona applies the precedence Stage 1c resolved rather than re-deriving it, and reads the files itself, targeting only relevant sections.
- Empty successful search: do not dispatch `project-standards`; record `project standards: not run (no applicable standards files)` in Coverage.
- Search failure or uncertain scope: dispatch `project-standards` with the uncertainty stated.

### Stage 3c: Depth already decided

The Review depth gate in `references/modes-and-output.md` already chose lite, focused, or full, before this file was read. This stage does not size the run and does not shrink the roster. You are on the full spine. Continue to Stage 3d.

### Stage 3d: Bind the adversarial route and final roster

Complete this stage **before reading persona prompt assets, `references/dispatch-reviewers.md`, or entering Stage 4** (Dispatch reviewers). That reference's persona-file instructions are valid only once you have settled which single route covers the adversarial lens: the peer, or the in-process fallback. This stage makes that exclusive choice between a cross-model adversarial peer and the in-process `adversarial-reviewer`. Later stages use that choice and must not decide it again, except when the fold-in step finds the peer never ran, or restores the in-process reviewer after a retry on the same route fails on a rate limit.

Both routes share the run directory Stage 1b created; do not create another.

When adversarial was selected and the working tree is the reviewed head (standalone, `base:`, or `local-aligned` scope), read `references/cross-model-review.md` from this skill's directory in full, verify the host as that reference requires, resolve one fixed route and approve it, and make the announcement that reference requires before anything is sent to the peer (its egress announcement, which tells the user what leaves the machine). Before start, write both inputs the reference defines; you, the orchestrator, write them, not the peer. They are the dedicated host-vetted constraints file, and the separate untrusted semantic brief containing intent plus material risk divisions inferred from the current file inventory and diff. Do not embed the diff, mechanically copy every path, or combine the two files. Then start the detached peer job using the reference's exact invocation and persist its job ID, target, requested model/reasoning, and start epoch in working state, recording `--start peer` in the stage log (`references/scope.md`) in that same shell call.

- If the runner returns a job ID, the peer covers the adversarial lens for this run. Remove `adversarial-reviewer` from the local roster immediately. Do not read its local persona asset or dispatch it later — except when the owning fold-in rules in `references/cross-model-recovery.md` require the did-not-run fallback or the in-process restore after a failed same-route rate-limit retry.
- If no job starts because of a dispatch-infrastructure failure (a non-zero exit before any job id, an unresolved `$SKILL_DIR`/script path), read `references/cross-model-recovery.md` at that point and first attempt its bounded same-route hand recovery before accepting the fallback. Re-run the identical resolved route, holding target/model and read scope fixed. Keep retrying only while each failure is a new, plausibly recoverable one and the shared peer deadline holds. If recovery returns a job id, treat it as the branch above (the peer covers the lens; remove `adversarial-reviewer`). Keep `adversarial-reviewer` in the local roster as the fallback, and record the peer skip reason for Coverage, only in two cases: recovery is exhausted (a failure repeats or the deadline is spent), or the peer was never eligible to start (selection condition not met, disabled by checkout config, host un-attestable, no different provider, or CLI missing).
- In `pr-remote` / `branch-remote`, do not start the peer; keep the selected in-process adversarial reviewer because it can inspect the reviewed refs.

When a job ID is returned and task tracking is active, add a distinct task that names the independent cross-model adversarial review. Keep it in progress while the detached job runs, then record its terminal outcome when the artifact is collected. Never create this task before a peer starts or leave it behind when the local adversarial fallback runs.

Do not proceed until the final local roster is settled. This is a hard rule, not a preference: a started peer and the in-process adversarial reviewer must never both receive the same review brief.

Announce that final team before spawning, as a user-facing summary: name the always-on reviewers plainly, and for each conditional reviewer give the one-line reason it was added (the real concern, not the keyword that matched). Do **not** put local reviewer model-tier labels (`[session model]`/`[mid-tier]`) or the internal names of the scope modes in this announcement — those are internal. Still decide each local reviewer's tier here and keep it in working state for Stage 4. The cross-model line is separate; it uses the model/reasoning and route wording from its reference, which depends on what the peer's receipt attests. This is progress reporting, not a blocking confirmation.

+"""Review 010 probes. All generated output stays in TemporaryDirectory.
+
+Normal invocation: py -m unittest handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_010.py
+bootstrap_unittest uses the existing runtime if the Windows launcher cannot find it.
+It does not install Python, change the registry, or change existing tests.
+"""
+
+import copy
+import csv
+from dataclasses import replace
+from fractions import Fraction
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import types
+import unittest
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
+
+from src.equipment import load_equipment
+from src.evaluate import aggregate, run_metrics, select_rule
+from src.generate import Generator, generate, load_setup
+from src.recipe import load_recipe
+from src.recipe_rules import EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS, RecipeProcess
+from src.summary import build_analysis, build_summary
+from src.trace import build_trace
+
+RECIPE = ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json"
+CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes/generator_restart.json"
+EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes/equipment_restart.json"
+BASE = "400081f9735cc584ac5299e72c75a7515fca57d9"
+
+
+def bootstrap_unittest(arguments):
+    """Run the requested py command using a temporary alias of this interpreter."""
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="review010-runtime-") as directory:
+        target = Path(directory)
+        shutil.copy2(sys.executable, target / "py.exe")
+        dll = "python%d%d.dll" % sys.version_info[:2]
+        shutil.copy2(Path(sys.base_prefix) / dll, target / dll)
+        env = {**os.environ, "PYTHONHOME": sys.base_prefix,
+               "PATH": str(target) + os.pathsep + sys.base_prefix + os.pathsep + os.environ.get("PATH", ""),
+               "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"}
+        print("Temporary py.exe alias -> " + sys.executable, flush=True)
+        print("Command: py " + " ".join(arguments), flush=True)
+        result = subprocess.run([str(target / "py.exe"), *arguments], cwd=ROOT, env=env)
+        return result.returncode
+
+
+def row(seq, pid, event, inspection="", result="", model="", equipment=""):
+    return dict(zip(EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS, (str(seq), pid, event, inspection, result, model, equipment)))
+
+
+def prepared(names):
+    result = []
+    for pid in names:
+        for event, model in (("REGISTER", "B"), ("P1", ""), ("P2", "")):
+            result.append(row(len(result) + 1, pid, event, model=model))
+    return result
+
+
+def leak(seq, pid, result="FAIL", equipment="LEAK-1"):
+    return row(seq, pid, "INSPECT", "LEAK", result, equipment=equipment)
+
+
+def restart(seq, equipment="LEAK-1", result="NO_FAULT", **kw):
+    return row(seq, kw.pop("pid", ""), "EQUIP_RESTART", result=result, equipment=equipment, **kw)
+
+
+def cli(directory, module, *arguments, timeout=20):
+    return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", module, *map(str, arguments)], cwd=directory,
+                          capture_output=True, timeout=timeout,
+                          env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8",
+                               "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"})
+
+
+class Probe010(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls):
+        cls.models = load_recipe(RECIPE).models
+        cls.book = load_equipment(EQUIPMENT, cls.models)
+
+    def test_p01_overlapping_format_errors_then_valid_restart(self):
+        process = RecipeProcess(self.models, self.book)
+        for item in prepared("abc") + [leak(1, "a"), leak(1, "b")]:
+            self.assertTrue(process.apply(item).accepted)
+        bad = restart(0, equipment="MISSING", result="fixed", pid="a", inspection="LEAK")
+        for expected, key, value in (
+            ("seq는 양의 정수여야 함", "seq", "1"),
+            ("EQUIP_RESTART의 product_id는 빈칸이어야 함", "product_id", ""),
+            ("EQUIP_RESTART의 inspection과 model은 빈칸이어야 함", "inspection", ""),
+            ("잘못된 재가동 결과 (FIXED 또는 NO_FAULT 필요)", "result", "FIXED"),
+            ("등록되지 않은 설비", "equipment", "LEAK-1"),
+        ):
+            before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment))
+            decision = process.apply(bad)
+            self.assertFalse(decision.accepted)
+            self.assertEqual(decision.reason, expected)
+            self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
+            bad[key] = value
+        self.assertTrue(process.apply(bad).accepted)
+        before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment))
+        self.assertEqual(process.apply(bad).reason, "가동 중인 설비 재가동")
+        self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
+        self.assertTrue(process.apply(leak(1, "c", "PASS")).accepted)
+
+    def test_p02_duplicate_nonmonotonic_seq_links_physical_stop(self):
+        events = prepared("abcdef") + [leak(90, "a"), leak(2, "b"), leak(2, "c", "PASS"),
+                  restart(1), leak(90, "d"), leak(2, "e"), leak(1, "f", "PASS"), restart(1)]
+        analysis = build_analysis(events, self.models, self.book)
+        self.assertEqual(len(analysis.stop_history), 2)
+        self.assertIs(analysis.stops["LEAK-1"], analysis.stop_history[0])
+        for pid, causes in (("c", "a;b"), ("f", "d;e")):
+            rejection = [x for x in build_trace(analysis, pid) if x["decision"] == "거절"]
+            self.assertEqual([x["cause_product_ids"] for x in rejection], [causes])
+        stops = [x for x in build_summary(analysis) if x["section"] == "equipment_stops" and "#" in x["key"]]
+        self.assertEqual([x["rejected_count"] for x in stops], [1, 1])
+        self.assertEqual([x["state"] for x in stops], ["재가동", "재가동"])
+
+    def test_p03_restart_disabled_case_and_blank_values(self):
+        for enabled in (False, True):
+            process = RecipeProcess(self.models, replace(self.book, restart_enabled=enabled))
+            for event, result, equipment in (("equip_restart", "FIXED", "LEAK-1"),
+                                             ("EQUIP_RESTART", "fixed", "LEAK-1"),
+                                             ("EQUIP_RESTART", "FIXED", "leak-1"),
+                                             ("EQUIP_RESTART", "", "")):
+                before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment))
+                decision = process.apply(row(1, "", event, result=result, equipment=equipment))
+                self.assertFalse(decision.accepted)
+                self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
+                if not enabled or event != "EQUIP_RESTART":
+                    self.assertEqual(decision.reason, "product_id가 비어 있음")
+
+    def test_p04_zero_delay_and_single_fail_threshold(self):
+        config, _, recipe, book = load_setup(CONFIG)
+        config = copy.deepcopy(config)
+        config.update(normal_fail_probability=0, defective_product_probability=0, error_rate=0)
+        config["faulty_equipment"] = {"LEAK-2": 1}
+        config["restart"] = {"delay_rows": 0}
+        book = replace(book, window=1, fails=1)
+        generator = Generator(42, config, recipe.models, book)
+        events = generator.normal_events(10)
+        analysis = build_analysis(events, recipe.models, book)
+        self.assertTrue(all(d.accepted for d in analysis.decisions))
+        self.assertEqual(len(analysis.stop_history), 1)
+        self.assertEqual(len(analysis.restarts), 1)
+        self.assertEqual(analysis.restarts[0].row_index, analysis.stop_history[0].row_index + 1)
+        self.assertEqual(analysis.restarts[0].result, "FIXED")
+        self.assertFalse(generator.pending_restarts)
+
+    def test_p05_missing_inspection_equipment_must_terminate(self):
+        # Equipment loader accepts a subset. Waiting cannot make a missing machine appear.
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            directory = Path(directory)
+            config = json.loads(CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+            config.update(recipe=str(RECIPE), equipment=str(directory / "equipment.json"), error_rate=0,
+                          normal_fail_probability=0, defective_product_probability=0,
+                          faulty_equipment={})
+            equipment = json.loads(EQUIPMENT.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+            equipment["equipment"] = {"LEAK-1": "LEAK"}
+            (directory / "equipment.json").write_text(json.dumps(equipment), encoding="utf-8")
+            (directory / "config.json").write_text(json.dumps(config), encoding="utf-8")
+            self.assertEqual(load_equipment(directory / "equipment.json", self.models).equipment, {"LEAK-1": "LEAK"})
+            try:
+                result = cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--config", directory / "config.json",
+                             "--products", "2", "--seeds", "0-0", "--rules", "3:2", "--out", "missing", timeout=4)
+            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+                self.fail("Valid equipment subset leaves generator waiting forever without any pending restart (4 s timeout)")
+            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
+            self.assertFalse((directory / "reports").exists())
+
+    def test_p06_multiple_faults_detection_rate_is_per_run(self):
+        events = prepared("ab") + [leak(20, "a", equipment="LEAK-2"), leak(21, "b", equipment="LEAK-2")]
+        metrics = run_metrics(build_analysis(events, self.models, self.book), {"LEAK-2", "LEAK-3"}, set())
+        actual = aggregate([metrics], 2)["detection_rate"]
+        self.assertEqual(actual, 1, "One of one runs detected faulty equipment; spec denominator is runs, not equipment-runs")
+
+    def test_p07_legacy_step_named_restart_preserves_generation(self):
+        source = subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", BASE + ":src/generate.py"], cwd=ROOT).decode("utf-8")
+        legacy = types.ModuleType("src._probe010_baseline_generate")
+        legacy.__file__ = str(ROOT / "src/generate.py")
+        exec(compile(source, legacy.__file__, "exec"), legacy.__dict__)
+        config, _, recipe, book = load_setup(ROOT / "recipes/generator.json")
+        models = {name: replace(model, steps=("EQUIP_RESTART",) + model.steps) for name, model in recipe.models.items()}
+        old = legacy.Generator(42, config, models, book)
+        new = Generator(42, config, models, book)
+        old_normal, new_normal = old.normal_events(60), new.normal_events(60)
+        self.assertEqual(new_normal, old_normal)
+        old_rows, old_counts = old.inject_errors(old_normal)
+        new_rows, new_counts = new.inject_errors(new_normal)
+        self.assertEqual(new_counts, old_counts)
+        mismatch = next((i for i, pair in enumerate(zip(old_rows, new_rows), 1) if pair[0] != pair[1]), None)
+        self.assertIsNone(mismatch, "Restart disabled, valid legacy process ID changes generated CSV at row %s" % mismatch)
+
+    def test_p08_selection_uses_unrounded_boundary_values(self):
+        def m(detection, delay, false):
+            return dict(detection_rate=Fraction(detection), mean_delay=Fraction(delay),
+                        mean_false_stops=Fraction(false), unfinished_median=Fraction(20))
+        self.assertEqual(select_rule({(3, 2): m("0.94999", "9", "0"),
+                                      (5, 2): m("0.95", "10", "1")}), (5, 2))
+        self.assertEqual(select_rule({(3, 2): m("1", "10.00001", "0"),
+                                      (5, 2): m("1", "10", "1")}), (5, 2))
+        self.assertEqual(select_rule({(3, 2): m("1", "9", "1.004"),
+                                      (5, 2): m("1", "9", "1.003")}), (5, 2))
+
+    def test_p09_existing_samples_seed42_and_reports_identical(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            directory = Path(directory)
+            for stem, options in (
+                ("sample_events", ()),
+                ("sample_events_v2", ("--recipe", ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models.json")),
+                ("sample_events_retest", ("--recipe", RECIPE)),
+                ("sample_events_equipment", ("--recipe", RECIPE, "--equipment", ROOT / "recipes/equipment.json")),
+            ):
+                result = cli(directory, "src.main", ROOT / "data" / (stem + ".csv"), *options)
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+            generated = directory / "gen_seed42.csv"
+            generate(42, 500, generated, ROOT / "recipes/generator.json")
+            for suffix in ("", ".meta.json"):
+                self.assertEqual(Path(str(generated) + suffix).read_bytes(),
+                                 (ROOT / "data/generated" / ("gen_seed42.csv" + suffix)).read_bytes())
+            result = cli(directory, "src.main", generated, "--recipe", RECIPE,
+                         "--equipment", ROOT / "recipes/equipment_generated.json", "--summary",
+                         "--trace", "GEN_0005", "--trace", "GEN_0030")
+            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+            reports = list((directory / "reports").iterdir())
+            self.assertEqual(len(reports), 20)
+            for report in reports:
+                with self.subTest(report=report.name):
+                    self.assertEqual(report.read_bytes(), (ROOT / "reports" / report.name).read_bytes())
+            print("P09: 20 reports + seed42 CSV/meta byte-identical", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p10_restart_csv_meta_and_reports_reproducible(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            directory = Path(directory)
+            for seed, products in ((42, 500), (7, 200)):

+                                 "--summary", "--trace", "GEN_0001")
+                    self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+                    snapshots.append({p.relative_to(work).as_posix(): p.read_bytes() for p in work.rglob("*") if p.is_file()})
+                self.assertEqual(snapshots[0], snapshots[1])
+                print("P10: seed=%d products=%d CSV/meta/6 reports byte-identical" % (seed, products), flush=True)

+def restart_delay(config, book):
+    if "restart" not in config:
+        return None
+    restart = config["restart"]
+    if not book.restart_enabled:
+        raise ValueError("restart 설정에는 restart_enabled 설비 파일이 필요함")
+    if (not isinstance(restart, dict) or set(restart) != {"delay_rows"}
+            or type(restart["delay_rows"]) is not int or restart["delay_rows"] < 0):
+        raise ValueError("restart는 0 이상의 정수 delay_rows만 가져야 함")

 `tests/test_generate.py`는 시드 42/500개와 7/200개에서 CSV·meta·기존 보고서 4종·summary·trace 재현성, 완료 제품의 모든 공정/PASS, 정지·폐기 뒤 허용 금지, 거절 합계, **모든 행마다 판정기를 deepcopy한 뒤 거절 전후 전체 제품·설비 상태 동일성**을 검사합니다. 500개 검사도 생략하지 않았습니다. `tests/test_summary.py`는 손계산 4제품 fixture(T45), 정지·입력 오류 분리(T46), 물리 순서와 교차 제품 trace(T47), 잘못된 옵션·없는 ID의 무출력 파일(T48), 미등록 이유 실패(T49)를 검사합니다. 기존 T1–T41 테스트 4개 파일과 `src/rules.py`는 수정하지 않았고 기존 샘플 보고서 13개도 재생성 바이트 비교를 통과했습니다.
+
+## 설비 복구와 정지 규칙 평가 (v2 5단계)
+
+정지만 있던 설비 모델에 점검 후 재가동을 추가하고, 정지 규칙을 여러 시드의 수치로 평가합니다. 이 단계부터 구현은 Claude Code, 읽기 전용 검수는 Codex(`gpt-6-astra`)가 맡습니다.
+
+**생성 데이터의 확률은 가정이며 실제 설비 진단 근거가 아니다.** 점검 소요·수리 효과도 가정입니다.
+
+### 재가동
+
+설비 파일에 `"restart_enabled": true`가 있을 때만 켜집니다. 없으면 이전 단계의 입력과 출력이 그대로입니다. 7열 CSV에 새 이벤트 하나를 씁니다.
+
+```csv
+seq,product_id,event,inspection,result,model,equipment
+24,,EQUIP_RESTART,,NO_FAULT,,LEAK-1
+```
+
+`result`는 `FIXED`(이상 발견·조치) 또는 `NO_FAULT`(이상 없음)이고 `product_id`, `inspection`, `model`은 빈칸입니다. 재가동은 정지 설비에만 허용합니다. 재가동하면 정지 판단 창을 비웁니다. 누적 건수는 유지합니다. 보류 해제나 열린 재검사 요청 소진은 하지 않습니다. 새 거절 사유 코드는 `EQUIP_NOT_STOPPED`, `PRESERVE_RESTART_PRODUCT`, `PRESERVE_RESTART_FIELDS`, `PRESERVE_BAD_RESTART_RESULT`입니다.
+
+```powershell
+py -m src.main data/sample_events_restart.csv --recipe recipes/lamp_models_retest.json --equipment recipes/equipment_restart.json --summary --trace f
+```
+
+재가동 모드의 equipment 보고서에는 `stop_count,restart_count,last_restart_result`가 붙습니다. summary의 `equipment_stops`는 정지 1건당 1행(`LEAK-1#1`)이고 `equipment_restarts` 절이 추가됩니다. trace의 정지 거절 행은 그 행 시점에 유효했던 정지를 가리킵니다.
+
+### 생성기 가정
+
+`recipes/generator_restart.json`은 기본 설정에 `"restart": {"delay_rows": 30}`을 더합니다. 설비가 멈추면 30행 뒤 재가동 행을 넣습니다. 같은 검사 설비가 모두 멈추면 제품은 끝나지 않고 기다립니다. 점검은 항상 맞다고 가정해 수리되지 않은 고장 설비는 `FIXED`, 나머지는 `NO_FAULT`입니다. `FIXED` 뒤 그 설비의 FAIL 확률은 정상값입니다. 생성기 버전은 재가동 설정에서만 `1.2.0`입니다.
+
+**한계:** 실제 시각이 없습니다. 제품이 같은 라운드에 같은 공정을 함께 지나가는 동시 투입 구조라, 행 수는 비가동 시간이 아닙니다. 고장은 생산 시작부터 존재합니다.
+
+### 평가
+
+```powershell
+py -m src.evaluate --seeds 0-99 --rules 3:2,5:2,5:3,10:3,10:4,20:4 --out eval_dev
+py -m src.evaluate --seeds 1000-1099 --rules 3:2 --out eval_holdout --holdout
+py -m src.evaluate --config recipes/generator.json --seeds 0-99 --rules 3:2 --out eval_baseline --holdout
+```
+
+`--holdout`은 규칙을 고르지 않고 기준 충족만 표시합니다. 정탐은 정지 시점에 수리되지 않은 고장 설비의 정지, 나머지는 오탐입니다. 검출 지연은 고장 설비의 첫 정지까지 처리한 최초 검사 수입니다. 미완료는 완료가 아닌 제품 전체로 보류·폐기를 포함합니다.
+
+선택 기준은 결과를 보기 전에 고정했습니다(`handoff/decisions.md`, 2026-10-30): 검출률 0.95 이상, 평균 지연 10 이하 규칙 중 오탐 평균이 가장 낮은 규칙. 시드 1000–1099는 확인에만 씁니다.
+
+### 평가 결과
+
+재가동 설정, 제품 500개, 시드 0–99입니다.
+
+| 규칙 | 검출률 | 평균 지연 | 정지 정밀도 | 오탐 평균 | 전면 정지 실행 | 기준 |
+| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
+| 3:2 | 1.0000 | 9.71 | 0.3448 | 1.90 | 3 | **충족, 선택** |
+| 5:2 | 1.0000 | 7.84 | 0.2227 | 3.49 | 6 | 충족 |
+| 10:3 | 1.0000 | 11.31 | 0.5291 | 0.89 | 0 | 지연 초과 |
+| 20:4 | 1.0000 | 13.64 | 0.6623 | 0.51 | 0 | 지연 초과 |
+| 10:4 | 1.0000 | 17.40 | 0.9346 | 0.07 | 0 | 지연 초과 |
+| 5:3 | 1.0000 | 19.77 | 0.8772 | 0.14 | 0 | 지연 초과 |
+
+- 사전 기준으로는 현행 3:2가 선택됩니다. 홀드아웃(시드 1000–1099)에서도 검출률 1.0000, 평균 지연 8.47로 기준을 유지했습니다(`확인`).
+- 선택됐다고 오탐 문제가 해결된 것은 아닙니다. 3:2의 정지 정밀도는 약 0.34로, 정지 3건 중 2건가량이 오탐입니다. 오탐 평균을 낮추려면 검출 지연을 늘려야 하는 교환 관계가 수치로 드러났습니다.
+- 재가동 효과(3:2, 시드 0–99): 전면 정지 실행은 49회에서 3회로, 미완료 최대는 417개에서 26개로 줄었습니다. 대신 결함품 완료 평균은 0.16에서 0.30으로 늘었습니다. 재가동 없이는 멈춰 있던 제품까지 끝까지 흐르기 때문으로 보이며, 원인은 별도로 검증하지 않았습니다.
+- 평가는 규칙을 제안만 합니다. 설비 파일의 `stop_rule`은 바꾸지 않았습니다.
+
+### 이번 단계 제외 범위
+
+실제 시각·UPH·OEE, 공정 설비, 로트 ID, 측정값·Cpk, 그래프, 생산 중간에 시작되는 고장, 재가동 취소는 포함하지 않습니다.

#Requires -Version 7
<#
Codex 검토 → Claude 수정 자동 반복 루프.

  pwsh -File tools/review-loop.ps1            # 수정 최대 3회
  pwsh -File tools/review-loop.ps1 -MaxFixes 1

한 회차 = Codex 검토 → (VERDICT: FIX이면) Claude 수정 → 스크립트가 커밋.
마지막 수정 뒤에는 확인용 검토를 한 번 더 한다. 그래서 검토는 최대 MaxFixes+1회다.

멈추는 조건
  - Codex 판정이 VERDICT: PASS (미해결 높음/중간 없음)
  - 수정 횟수가 MaxFixes에 도달
  - Claude가 아무 파일도 바꾸지 않음 (진전 없음)
  - Codex가 리뷰 파일·반례 파일 밖을 변경함 (커밋하지 않고 멈춤)
  - 판정 줄이 없거나 CLI가 실패함

Claude와 Codex는 커밋하지 않는다. 커밋은 이 스크립트가 한다.
사용자 승인(handoff/decisions.md)은 루프가 끝난 뒤 사람이 기록한다.
#>
param(
    [ValidateRange(0, 10)]
    [int]$MaxFixes = 3,
    # 검토에 쓰는 Codex 모델. 플랜에서 쓸 수 없으면 Codex 실행 실패로 멈춘다.
    [string]$ReviewModel = 'gpt-6-astra',
    # 수정에 쓰는 Claude 모델.
    [string]$FixModel = 'claude-opus-5-5'
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
[Console]::OutputEncoding = [Text.Encoding]::UTF8
$OutputEncoding = [Text.Encoding]::UTF8

$root = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
Set-Location $root
$reviewDir = Join-Path $root 'handoff/reviews'
$logDir = Join-Path $root 'handoff/loop-logs'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $reviewDir, $logDir | Out-Null

function Stop-Loop([string]$message) {
    Write-Host "`n[루프 종료] $message" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    exit 0
}

function Assert-Command([string]$name) {
    if (-not (Get-Command $name -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
        throw "$name 명령을 찾을 수 없습니다. npm 전역 설치와 PATH를 확인하세요."
    }
}

function Get-NextReviewNumber {
    $nums = Get-ChildItem $reviewDir -Filter '*-review.md' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        ForEach-Object { if ($_.Name -match '^(\d{3})-review\.md$') { [int]$Matches[1] } }
    $max = ($nums | Measure-Object -Maximum).Maximum
    return [int]$max + 1
}

function Get-Verdict([string]$path) {
    if (-not (Test-Path $path)) { return $null }
    $line = Select-String -Path $path -Pattern '^\s*VERDICT:\s*(PASS|FIX)\s*$' | Select-Object -Last 1
    if ($line) { return $line.Matches[0].Groups[1].Value }
    return $null
}

function Save-Commit([string]$message, [string[]]$paths) {
    git add -- @paths
    git diff --cached --quiet
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        git commit -q -m $message
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "커밋 실패: $message" }
    }
}

function Test-Dirty {
    # 루프 자신의 로그(실패한 실행이 남긴 것 포함)는 변경으로 치지 않는다.
    [bool](git status --porcelain -- . ':(exclude)handoff/loop-logs')
}

Assert-Command 'claude'
Assert-Command 'codex'
Assert-Command 'git'

if (Test-Dirty) {
    Stop-Loop '작업 트리에 커밋하지 않은 변경이 있습니다. 구현 작업을 먼저 커밋한 뒤 다시 실행하세요.'
}

# 첫 검토 범위: 마지막 "review:" 커밋 이후 전체. 없으면 저장소 처음부터.
$baseCommit = (git log --grep '^review:' -n 1 --format=%H | Out-String).Trim()
if (-not $baseCommit) { $baseCommit = (git rev-list --max-parents=0 HEAD | Select-Object -First 1).Trim() }
$prevReviewCommit = $null
$prevReviewFile = $null
$fixes = 0

while ($true) {
    $num = '{0:D3}' -f (Get-NextReviewNumber)
    $reviewFile = "handoff/reviews/$num-review.md"
    $round = $fixes + 1
    Write-Host "`n=== 검토 $num (회차 $round, 수정 $fixes/$MaxFixes) ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan

    if ($prevReviewCommit) {
        $scope = @"
이번은 재검토다. 이전 검토 파일은 $prevReviewFile 이다.
변경 범위는 git diff $prevReviewCommit..HEAD 로 확인한다.
- 이전 높음/중간 항목이 해결됐는지 항목별로 판정한다.
- handoff/gpt-log.md의 반박이 근거 있으면 받아들이고 해결로 처리한다.
- 수정이 새 문제를 만들지 않았는지 반례로 다시 확인한다. 새 지적도 중간 이상이면 올린다.
"@
    } else {
        $scope = "첫 검토다. 변경 범위는 git diff $baseCommit..HEAD 와 git log $baseCommit..HEAD 로 확인한다(직전 검토 이후 커밋 전체)."
    }

    $reviewPrompt = @"
AGENTS.md의 검수자 규칙을 따른다. 자동 검토 루프의 검토 단계다.
handoff/tasks.md 맨 위 「현행 작업」의 명세와 기대표를 기준으로 코드를 검수하라.
이전 단계들의 보존 조건(기존 테스트 무수정 통과, 기존 보고서 동일)도 반드시 확인한다.
$scope
- 테스트는 py -m unittest discover -s tests 로 직접 실행하고 명령과 결과를 붙인다.
- AGENTS.md의 「반례 탐색」대로 명세 밖 반례를 최소 5개 handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_$num.py 에 쓰고 실행한다.
- 결과는 $reviewFile 와 handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_$num.py 에만 쓴다. 다른 파일은 만들거나 고치지 않는다.
- 심각도는 AGENTS.md의 기준(영향)으로만 정한다. 마감·일정은 심각도를 낮추는 근거가 아니다. 낮음은 기록만 하고 수정 요구로 세지 않는다.
- 파일의 마지막 줄에는 반드시 아래 둘 중 하나만 단독으로 쓴다.
  VERDICT: PASS   (미해결 높음/중간이 없을 때)
  VERDICT: FIX    (미해결 높음/중간이 하나라도 있을 때)
"@

    $reviewLog = Join-Path $logDir "$num-codex.txt"
    $reviewPrompt | codex exec -C $root -m $ReviewModel -s workspace-write - 2>&1 | Tee-Object -FilePath $reviewLog
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Stop-Loop "Codex 실행 실패 (종료 코드 $LASTEXITCODE). 로그: $reviewLog" }

    # 샌드박스는 파일 단위로 막지 못하므로 검수자의 쓰기 범위를 여기서 확인한다.
    $outside = git status --porcelain -- . ':(exclude)handoff/loop-logs' ':(exclude)handoff/reviews'
    if ($outside) { Stop-Loop "검수자가 리뷰 파일·반례 파일 밖을 변경했습니다. 커밋하지 않았습니다:`n$($outside -join "`n")" }

    $verdict = Get-Verdict (Join-Path $root $reviewFile)
    if (-not $verdict) {
        Save-Commit "review: $num (verdict missing)" @('handoff')
        Stop-Loop "$reviewFile 에 VERDICT 줄이 없습니다. 파일을 직접 확인하세요."
    }
    Save-Commit "review: $num ($verdict)" @('handoff')
    $prevReviewCommit = (git rev-parse HEAD).Trim()
    $prevReviewFile = $reviewFile
    Write-Host "판정: $verdict" -ForegroundColor Green

    if ($verdict -eq 'PASS') { Stop-Loop "검토 $num 통과. handoff/decisions.md에 승인 여부를 기록하세요." }
    if ($fixes -ge $MaxFixes) { Stop-Loop "수정 상한($MaxFixes회)에 도달했습니다. 남은 항목은 $reviewFile 을 보고 직접 결정하세요." }

    Write-Host "`n=== Claude 수정 (리뷰 $num 반영, $($fixes + 1)/$MaxFixes) ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    $fixPrompt = @"
CLAUDE.md의 구현 담당 규칙을 따른다. 자동 검토 루프의 수정 단계다.
$reviewFile 의 높음/중간 항목만 처리하라. 낮음 항목과 새 기능은 건드리지 않는다.
- 항목마다 반영/보류/반박과 이유를 handoff/gpt-log.md에 기록한다. 리뷰가 틀렸다면 근거를 들어 반박해도 된다.
- handoff/tasks.md의 기대표와 기존 테스트의 기대값을 결과에 맞춰 바꾸지 않는다.
- handoff/reviews/, handoff/decisions.md, AGENTS.md, .claude/ 는 수정하지 않는다.
- 수정 후 py -m unittest discover -s tests 를 실행하고 결과를 gpt-log.md에 붙인다.
- git commit 은 하지 않는다. 커밋은 루프 스크립트가 한다.
"@

    $fixLog = Join-Path $logDir "$num-claude.txt"
    $fixPrompt | claude -p --model $FixModel --permission-mode dontAsk 2>&1 | Tee-Object -FilePath $fixLog
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Stop-Loop "Claude 실행 실패 (종료 코드 $LASTEXITCODE). 로그: $fixLog" }

    if (-not (Test-Dirty)) { Stop-Loop 'Claude가 아무 파일도 바꾸지 않았습니다. 진전이 없어 멈춥니다.' }
    $fixes++
    Save-Commit "fix: apply review $num (loop $fixes/$MaxFixes)" @('.')
}


codex
반례에서 재가동을 꺼도 기존 출력이 달라지는 보존 위반을 확인했습니다. 특정 검사 설비가 목록에 없을 때 생성기가 무한 대기하는 문제도 재현됐습니다. 기존 샘플·seed42 보고서 20개는 바이트까지 같았으며, 평가 지표의 누락과 명세 해석 차이를 추가 대조하고 있습니다.

+"""Review 010 probes. All generated output stays in TemporaryDirectory.
+
+Normal invocation: py -m unittest handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_010.py
+bootstrap_unittest uses the existing runtime if the Windows launcher cannot find it.
+It does not install Python, change the registry, or change existing tests.
+"""
+
+import copy
+import csv
+from dataclasses import replace
+from fractions import Fraction
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import types
+import unittest
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
+
+from src.equipment import load_equipment
+from src.evaluate import aggregate, run_metrics, select_rule
+from src.generate import Generator, generate, load_setup
+from src.recipe import load_recipe
+from src.recipe_rules import EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS, RecipeProcess
+from src.summary import build_analysis, build_summary
+from src.trace import build_trace
+
+RECIPE = ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json"
+CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes/generator_restart.json"
+EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes/equipment_restart.json"
+BASE = "400081f9735cc584ac5299e72c75a7515fca57d9"
+
+
+def bootstrap_unittest(arguments):
+    """Run the requested py command using a temporary alias of this interpreter."""
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="review010-runtime-") as directory:
+        target = Path(directory)
+        shutil.copy2(sys.executable, target / "py.exe")
+        dll = "python%d%d.dll" % sys.version_info[:2]
+        shutil.copy2(Path(sys.base_prefix) / dll, target / dll)
+        env = {**os.environ, "PYTHONHOME": sys.base_prefix,
+               "PATH": str(target) + os.pathsep + sys.base_prefix + os.pathsep + os.environ.get("PATH", ""),
+               "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"}
+        print("Temporary py.exe alias -> " + sys.executable, flush=True)
+        print("Command: py " + " ".join(arguments), flush=True)
+        result = subprocess.run([str(target / "py.exe"), *arguments], cwd=ROOT, env=env)
+        return result.returncode
+
+
+def row(seq, pid, event, inspection="", result="", model="", equipment=""):
+    return dict(zip(EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS, (str(seq), pid, event, inspection, result, model, equipment)))
+
+
+def prepared(names):
+    result = []
+    for pid in names:
+        for event, model in (("REGISTER", "B"), ("P1", ""), ("P2", "")):
+            result.append(row(len(result) + 1, pid, event, model=model))
+    return result
+
+
+def leak(seq, pid, result="FAIL", equipment="LEAK-1"):
+    return row(seq, pid, "INSPECT", "LEAK", result, equipment=equipment)
+
+
+def restart(seq, equipment="LEAK-1", result="NO_FAULT", **kw):
+    return row(seq, kw.pop("pid", ""), "EQUIP_RESTART", result=result, equipment=equipment, **kw)
+
+
+def cli(directory, module, *arguments, timeout=20):
+    return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", module, *map(str, arguments)], cwd=directory,
+                          capture_output=True, timeout=timeout,
+                          env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8",
+                               "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"})
+
+
+class Probe010(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls):
+        cls.models = load_recipe(RECIPE).models
+        cls.book = load_equipment(EQUIPMENT, cls.models)
+
+    def test_p01_overlapping_format_errors_then_valid_restart(self):
+        process = RecipeProcess(self.models, self.book)
+        for item in prepared("abc") + [leak(1, "a"), leak(1, "b")]:
+            self.assertTrue(process.apply(item).accepted)
+        bad = restart(0, equipment="MISSING", result="fixed", pid="a", inspection="LEAK")
+        for expected, key, value in (
+            ("seq는 양의 정수여야 함", "seq", "1"),
+            ("EQUIP_RESTART의 product_id는 빈칸이어야 함", "product_id", ""),
+            ("EQUIP_RESTART의 inspection과 model은 빈칸이어야 함", "inspection", ""),
+            ("잘못된 재가동 결과 (FIXED 또는 NO_FAULT 필요)", "result", "FIXED"),
+            ("등록되지 않은 설비", "equipment", "LEAK-1"),
+        ):
+            before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment))
+            decision = process.apply(bad)
+            self.assertFalse(decision.accepted)
+            self.assertEqual(decision.reason, expected)
+            self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
+            bad[key] = value
+        self.assertTrue(process.apply(bad).accepted)
+        before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment))
+        self.assertEqual(process.apply(bad).reason, "가동 중인 설비 재가동")
+        self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
+        self.assertTrue(process.apply(leak(1, "c", "PASS")).accepted)
+
+    def test_p02_duplicate_nonmonotonic_seq_links_physical_stop(self):
+        events = prepared("abcdef") + [leak(90, "a"), leak(2, "b"), leak(2, "c", "PASS"),
+                  restart(1), leak(90, "d"), leak(2, "e"), leak(1, "f", "PASS"), restart(1)]
+        analysis = build_analysis(events, self.models, self.book)
+        self.assertEqual(len(analysis.stop_history), 2)
+        self.assertIs(analysis.stops["LEAK-1"], analysis.stop_history[0])
+        for pid, causes in (("c", "a;b"), ("f", "d;e")):
+            rejection = [x for x in build_trace(analysis, pid) if x["decision"] == "거절"]
+            self.assertEqual([x["cause_product_ids"] for x in rejection], [causes])
+        stops = [x for x in build_summary(analysis) if x["section"] == "equipment_stops" and "#" in x["key"]]
+        self.assertEqual([x["rejected_count"] for x in stops], [1, 1])
+        self.assertEqual([x["state"] for x in stops], ["재가동", "재가동"])
+
+    def test_p03_restart_disabled_case_and_blank_values(self):
+        for enabled in (False, True):
+            process = RecipeProcess(self.models, replace(self.book, restart_enabled=enabled))
+            for event, result, equipment in (("equip_restart", "FIXED", "LEAK-1"),
+                                             ("EQUIP_RESTART", "fixed", "LEAK-1"),
+                                             ("EQUIP_RESTART", "FIXED", "leak-1"),
+                                             ("EQUIP_RESTART", "", "")):
+                before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment))
+                decision = process.apply(row(1, "", event, result=result, equipment=equipment))
+                self.assertFalse(decision.accepted)
+                self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
+                if not enabled or event != "EQUIP_RESTART":
+                    self.assertEqual(decision.reason, "product_id가 비어 있음")

+            (directory / "equipment.json").write_text(json.dumps(equipment), encoding="utf-8")
+            (directory / "config.json").write_text(json.dumps(config), encoding="utf-8")
+            self.assertEqual(load_equipment(directory / "equipment.json", self.models).equipment, {"LEAK-1": "LEAK"})
+            try:
+                result = cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--config", directory / "config.json",
+                             "--products", "2", "--seeds", "0-0", "--rules", "3:2", "--out", "missing", timeout=4)
+            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+                self.fail("Valid equipment subset leaves generator waiting forever without any pending restart (4 s timeout)")

+    def test_p07_legacy_step_named_restart_preserves_generation(self):

+        self.assertEqual(new_counts, old_counts)
+        mismatch = next((i for i, pair in enumerate(zip(old_rows, new_rows), 1) if pair[0] != pair[1]), None)
+        self.assertIsNone(mismatch, "Restart disabled, valid legacy process ID changes generated CSV at row %s" % mismatch)

+    def test_p08_selection_uses_unrounded_boundary_values(self):
+        def m(detection, delay, false):
+            return dict(detection_rate=Fraction(detection), mean_delay=Fraction(delay),
+                        mean_false_stops=Fraction(false), unfinished_median=Fraction(20))
+        self.assertEqual(select_rule({(3, 2): m("0.94999", "9", "0"),
+                                      (5, 2): m("0.95", "10", "1")}), (5, 2))
+        self.assertEqual(select_rule({(3, 2): m("1", "10.00001", "0"),
+                                      (5, 2): m("1", "10", "1")}), (5, 2))
+        self.assertEqual(select_rule({(3, 2): m("1", "9", "1.004"),
+                                      (5, 2): m("1", "9", "1.003")}), (5, 2))

+    def test_p12_delay_rows_after_error_insertion(self):
+        config, _, recipe, book = load_setup(CONFIG)
+        generator = Generator(42, config, recipe.models, book)
+        normal = generator.normal_events(500)
+        rows, _ = generator.inject_errors(normal)
+        analysis = build_analysis(rows, recipe.models, book)
+        gaps = []
+        for resumed in analysis.restarts:
+            stop = analysis.stop_at(resumed.equipment, resumed.row_index)
+            intervening = resumed.row_index - stop.row_index - 1
+            gaps.append((resumed.equipment, stop.row_index, resumed.row_index, intervening))
+        self.assertTrue(gaps)
+        print("P12 final CSV stop/restart/intervening rows:", gaps, flush=True)
+        # The published definition says rows, with no exclusion for rejected injected rows.
+        self.assertTrue(all(gap[3] <= config["restart"]["delay_rows"] for gap in gaps),
+                        "A due restart must precede the next product row; error insertion delays it: %r" % gaps)

79: def aggregate(runs, faulty_count):
80:     count = len(runs)
81:     stops = sum(run.true_stops + run.false_stops for run in runs)
82:     detected = sum(run.detected for run in runs)
83:     unfinished = [run.unfinished for run in runs]
84:     return {
85:         "runs": count,
86:         "detection_rate": Fraction(detected, count * faulty_count) if faulty_count else None,
87:         "mean_delay": Fraction(sum(run.delay_sum for run in runs), detected) if detected else None,
88:         "stop_precision": Fraction(sum(run.true_stops for run in runs), stops) if stops else None,
89:         "mean_false_stops": Fraction(sum(run.false_stops for run in runs), count),
90:         "false_stop_runs": sum(run.false_stops > 0 for run in runs),
91:         "full_outage_runs": sum(run.full_outage for run in runs),
92:         "unfinished_min": min(unfinished), "unfinished_median": Fraction(median(unfinished)),
93:         "unfinished_max": max(unfinished),
94:         "mean_normal_held": Fraction(sum(run.normal_held for run in runs), count),
95:         "mean_defective_completed": Fraction(sum(run.defective_completed for run in runs), count),
96:     }

167: def _decimal(value, places):
168:     if value is None:
169:         return ""
170:     exact = Decimal(value.numerator) / Decimal(value.denominator)
171:     return format(exact.quantize(Decimal(1).scaleb(-places), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP), "." + str(places) + "f")

98:     def normal_events(self, products):

100:         weights = list(self.config["model_weights"].values())

# 인수인계: GPT–Claude 교차검수 환경 설정 (claude.ai 채팅 → Claude Code)

작성일: 2026-10-17 (같은 날 주제·위치 변경 반영)

## 배경
- 에스엘(SL) KDT AI 부트캠프 지원용 프로젝트이며 마감은 2026-10-18이다.
- 프로젝트 주제: 「교육용 가상 조립·검사 공정의 진행 조건 판정기」(Python CLI). 확정 명세와 기대표 T1–T10은 `handoff/tasks.md`에 있다.
  - 주제 변경 이력: 램프 조립·검사 진행·보류·재검토(v6) → 명령–응답 로그 검증(검토만 하고 폐기) → 현재 판정기. 재검사 범위는 이번 버전에서 제외한다.
- 역할 분담:
  - GPT(Codex): 구현과 수정을 맡는다.
  - Claude(Claude Code): 읽기 전용 교차검토와 피드백만 맡는다.
  - 사용자: 요구사항 제시, 최종 승인·반려, 결과 설명과 시연을 맡는다.
- 목표: 작성 AI와 읽기 전용 검수 AI를 분리하고 승인 기록과 증거를 남기는 멀티 에이전트 워크플로우를 보여준다.
- 환경: Windows 11, ChatGPT Plus, Claude Pro. 유료 API는 사용하지 않는다. Python은 `py`(3.9)로 실행한다. `python`은 Store 안내로 연결된다.

## 확정한 구조
- 코드 저장소: `C:\Users\PC\Documents\ChatGPT\에스엘 KDT AI 교육 부트캠프\project` (git, GitHub 원격 연결됨). Codex와 Claude Code 모두 이 폴더를 기본으로 연다.
- 상위 폴더의 `outputs\` 등 문서는 저장소 밖이며 검수 대상이 아니다.
- `docs/`의 기존 문서(v6, 재검사 R1–R8 등)는 이전 범위 기록이다. 현행 기준은 `handoff/tasks.md`와 README다.

```
project/
├─ AGENTS.md            Codex 규칙
├─ CLAUDE.md            Claude Code 규칙
├─ .claude/settings.json
├─ src/, tests/, data/  Codex만 수정
├─ docs/                이전 범위 기록 (requirements.md 맨 위 한 줄만 Codex가 추가)
└─ handoff/
   ├─ tasks.md          확정 명세와 기대표
   ├─ gpt-log.md        Codex 변경 내역과 이유, 리뷰 반영 여부
   ├─ reviews/          Claude 검수 결과 (NNN-review.md)
   └─ decisions.md      사용자 승인·반려
```

## 작업 순서
1. 사용자가 `tasks.md`를 작성한다. (완료)
2. Codex가 구현하고 커밋한 뒤 `gpt-log.md`에 기록한다.
3. Claude가 검수하고 결과를 `reviews/`에 남긴다.
4. 사용자가 `decisions.md`에 승인 여부를 기록한다.
5. Codex가 리뷰를 반영한다. 이 과정을 반복한다.

## Claude 권한 원칙
- 허용: 전체 읽기, `git diff`·`git log`·`git status`, 테스트 실행(`py -m unittest`), `handoff/reviews/`에 쓰기
- 금지: `src/`·`tests/`·`AGENTS.md` 수정, commit·push, pip 설치, 파일 삭제
- 목록에 없는 명령은 실행 전에 승인을 묻는 상태로 유지한다. deny 규칙만으로는 셸을 통한 쓰기를 완전히 막을 수 없기 때문이다.

## Claude Code에서 이어서 할 일
- [x] 폴더 구조 확인과 검수 설정 배치 (2026-10-17, `project\`로 통합)
- [x] `handoff/` 폴더와 파일 생성, `tasks.md`에 확정 명세 기록 (2026-10-17)

- [ ] 첫 검수: Codex 완료 보고 뒤 `tasks.md` 기대표 T1–T10, `gpt-log.md`, 최근 git diff를 기준으로 진행하고 테스트를 직접 실행한다.

+"""Review 010 probes. All generated output stays in TemporaryDirectory.
+
+Normal invocation: py -m unittest handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_010.py
+bootstrap_unittest uses the existing runtime if the Windows launcher cannot find it.
+It does not install Python, change the registry, or change existing tests.
+"""
+
+import copy
+import csv
+from dataclasses import replace
+from fractions import Fraction
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import types
+import unittest
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
+
+from src.equipment import load_equipment
+from src.evaluate import aggregate, run_metrics, select_rule
+from src.generate import Generator, build_rows, generate, load_setup
+from src.recipe import load_recipe
+from src.recipe_rules import EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS, RecipeProcess
+from src.summary import build_analysis, build_summary
+from src.trace import build_trace
+
+RECIPE = ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json"
+CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes/generator_restart.json"
+EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes/equipment_restart.json"
+BASE = "400081f9735cc584ac5299e72c75a7515fca57d9"
+
+
+def bootstrap_unittest(arguments):
+    """Run the requested py command using a temporary alias of this interpreter."""
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="review010-runtime-") as directory:
+        target = Path(directory)
+        shutil.copy2(sys.executable, target / "py.exe")
+        dll = "python%d%d.dll" % sys.version_info[:2]
+        shutil.copy2(Path(sys.base_prefix) / dll, target / dll)
+        env = {**os.environ, "PYTHONHOME": sys.base_prefix,
+               "PATH": str(target) + os.pathsep + sys.base_prefix + os.pathsep + os.environ.get("PATH", ""),
+               "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"}
+        print("Temporary py.exe alias -> " + sys.executable, flush=True)
+        print("Command: py " + " ".join(arguments), flush=True)
+        result = subprocess.run([str(target / "py.exe"), *arguments], cwd=ROOT, env=env)
+        return result.returncode
+
+
+def row(seq, pid, event, inspection="", result="", model="", equipment=""):
+    return dict(zip(EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS, (str(seq), pid, event, inspection, result, model, equipment)))
+
+
+def prepared(names):
+    result = []
+    for pid in names:
+        for event, model in (("REGISTER", "B"), ("P1", ""), ("P2", "")):
+            result.append(row(len(result) + 1, pid, event, model=model))
+    return result
+
+
+def leak(seq, pid, result="FAIL", equipment="LEAK-1"):
+    return row(seq, pid, "INSPECT", "LEAK", result, equipment=equipment)
+
+
+def restart(seq, equipment="LEAK-1", result="NO_FAULT", **kw):
+    return row(seq, kw.pop("pid", ""), "EQUIP_RESTART", result=result, equipment=equipment, **kw)
+
+
+def cli(directory, module, *arguments, timeout=20):
+    return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", module, *map(str, arguments)], cwd=directory,
+                          capture_output=True, timeout=timeout,
+                          env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8",
+                               "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"})
+
+
+class Probe010(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls):
+        cls.models = load_recipe(RECIPE).models
+        cls.book = load_equipment(EQUIPMENT, cls.models)
+
+    def test_p01_overlapping_format_errors_then_valid_restart(self):
+        process = RecipeProcess(self.models, self.book)
+        for item in prepared("abc") + [leak(1, "a"), leak(1, "b")]:
+            self.assertTrue(process.apply(item).accepted)
+        bad = restart(0, equipment="MISSING", result="fixed", pid="a", inspection="LEAK")
+        for expected, key, value in (
+            ("seq는 양의 정수여야 함", "seq", "1"),
+            ("EQUIP_RESTART의 product_id는 빈칸이어야 함", "product_id", ""),
+            ("EQUIP_RESTART의 inspection과 model은 빈칸이어야 함", "inspection", ""),
+            ("잘못된 재가동 결과 (FIXED 또는 NO_FAULT 필요)", "result", "FIXED"),
+            ("등록되지 않은 설비", "equipment", "LEAK-1"),
+        ):
+            before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment))
+            decision = process.apply(bad)
+            self.assertFalse(decision.accepted)
+            self.assertEqual(decision.reason, expected)
+            self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
+            bad[key] = value
+        self.assertTrue(process.apply(bad).accepted)
+        before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment))
+        self.assertEqual(process.apply(bad).reason, "가동 중인 설비 재가동")
+        self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
+        self.assertTrue(process.apply(leak(1, "c", "PASS")).accepted)
+
+    def test_p02_duplicate_nonmonotonic_seq_links_physical_stop(self):
+        events = prepared("abcdef") + [leak(90, "a"), leak(2, "b"), leak(2, "c", "PASS"),
+                  restart(1), leak(90, "d"), leak(2, "e"), leak(1, "f", "PASS"), restart(1)]
+        analysis = build_analysis(events, self.models, self.book)
+        self.assertEqual(len(analysis.stop_history), 2)
+        self.assertIs(analysis.stops["LEAK-1"], analysis.stop_history[0])
+        for pid, causes in (("c", "a;b"), ("f", "d;e")):
+            rejection = [x for x in build_trace(analysis, pid) if x["decision"] == "거절"]
+            self.assertEqual([x["cause_product_ids"] for x in rejection], [causes])
+        stops = [x for x in build_summary(analysis) if x["section"] == "equipment_stops" and "#" in x["key"]]
+        self.assertEqual([x["rejected_count"] for x in stops], [1, 1])
+        self.assertEqual([x["state"] for x in stops], ["재가동", "재가동"])
+
+    def test_p03_restart_disabled_case_and_blank_values(self):
+        for enabled in (False, True):
+            process = RecipeProcess(self.models, replace(self.book, restart_enabled=enabled))
+            for event, result, equipment in (("equip_restart", "FIXED", "LEAK-1"),
+                                             ("EQUIP_RESTART", "fixed", "LEAK-1"),
+                                             ("EQUIP_RESTART", "FIXED", "leak-1"),
+                                             ("EQUIP_RESTART", "", "")):
+                before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment))
+                decision = process.apply(row(1, "", event, result=result, equipment=equipment))
+                self.assertFalse(decision.accepted)
+                self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
+                if not enabled or event != "EQUIP_RESTART":
+                    self.assertEqual(decision.reason, "product_id가 비어 있음")
+
+    def test_p04_zero_delay_and_single_fail_threshold(self):
+        config, _, recipe, book = load_setup(CONFIG)
+        config = copy.deepcopy(config)
+        config.update(normal_fail_probability=0, defective_product_probability=0, error_rate=0)
+        config["faulty_equipment"] = {"LEAK-2": 1}
+        config["restart"] = {"delay_rows": 0}
+        book = replace(book, window=1, fails=1)
+        generator = Generator(42, config, recipe.models, book)
+        events = generator.normal_events(10)
+        analysis = build_analysis(events, recipe.models, book)
+        self.assertTrue(all(d.accepted for d in analysis.decisions))
+        self.assertEqual(len(analysis.stop_history), 1)
+        self.assertEqual(len(analysis.restarts), 1)
+        self.assertEqual(analysis.restarts[0].row_index, analysis.stop_history[0].row_index + 1)
+        self.assertEqual(analysis.restarts[0].result, "FIXED")
+        self.assertFalse(generator.pending_restarts)
+
+    def test_p05_missing_inspection_equipment_must_terminate(self):
+        # Equipment loader accepts a subset. Waiting cannot make a missing machine appear.
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            directory = Path(directory)
+            config = json.loads(CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+            config.update(recipe=str(RECIPE), equipment=str(directory / "equipment.json"), error_rate=0,
+                          normal_fail_probability=0, defective_product_probability=0,
+                          faulty_equipment={})
+            equipment = json.loads(EQUIPMENT.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+            equipment["equipment"] = {"LEAK-1": "LEAK"}
+            (directory / "equipment.json").write_text(json.dumps(equipment), encoding="utf-8")
+            (directory / "config.json").write_text(json.dumps(config), encoding="utf-8")
+            self.assertEqual(load_equipment(directory / "equipment.json", self.models).equipment, {"LEAK-1": "LEAK"})
+            try:
+                result = cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--config", directory / "config.json",
+                             "--products", "2", "--seeds", "0-0", "--rules", "3:2", "--out", "missing", timeout=4)
+            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+                self.fail("Valid equipment subset leaves generator waiting forever without any pending restart (4 s timeout)")
+            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
+            self.assertFalse((directory / "reports").exists())
+
+    def test_p06_multiple_faults_detection_rate_is_per_run(self):
+        events = prepared("ab") + [leak(20, "a", equipment="LEAK-2"), leak(21, "b", equipment="LEAK-2")]
+        metrics = run_metrics(build_analysis(events, self.models, self.book), {"LEAK-2", "LEAK-3"}, set())
+        actual = aggregate([metrics], 2)["detection_rate"]
+        self.assertEqual(actual, 1, "One of one runs detected faulty equipment; spec denominator is runs, not equipment-runs")
+
+    def test_p07_legacy_step_named_restart_preserves_generation(self):
+        source = subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", BASE + ":src/generate.py"], cwd=ROOT).decode("utf-8")
+        legacy = types.ModuleType("src._probe010_baseline_generate")
+        legacy.__package__ = "src"
+        legacy.__file__ = str(ROOT / "src/generate.py")
+        exec(compile(source, legacy.__file__, "exec"), legacy.__dict__)
+        config, _, recipe, book = load_setup(ROOT / "recipes/generator.json")
+        models = {name: replace(model, steps=("EQUIP_RESTART",) + model.steps) for name, model in recipe.models.items()}
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            data = json.loads(RECIPE.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+            for model in data.values():
+                model["steps"].insert(0, "EQUIP_RESTART")
+            path = Path(directory) / "recipe.json"
+            path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
+            self.assertEqual(load_recipe(path).models, models)
+        old = legacy.Generator(42, config, models, book)
+        new = Generator(42, config, models, book)
+        old_normal, new_normal = old.normal_events(60), new.normal_events(60)
+        self.assertEqual(new_normal, old_normal)
+        old_rows, old_counts = old.inject_errors(old_normal)
+        new_rows, new_counts = new.inject_errors(new_normal)
+        self.assertEqual(new_counts, old_counts)
+        mismatch = next((i for i, pair in enumerate(zip(old_rows, new_rows), 1) if pair[0] != pair[1]), None)
+        self.assertIsNone(mismatch, "Restart disabled, valid legacy process ID changes generated CSV at row %s" % mismatch)
+
+    def test_p08_selection_uses_unrounded_boundary_values(self):
+        def m(detection, delay, false):
+            return dict(detection_rate=Fraction(detection), mean_delay=Fraction(delay),
+                        mean_false_stops=Fraction(false), unfinished_median=Fraction(20))
+        self.assertEqual(select_rule({(3, 2): m("0.94999", "9", "0"),
+                                      (5, 2): m("0.95", "10", "1")}), (5, 2))
+        self.assertEqual(select_rule({(3, 2): m("1", "10.00001", "0"),
+                                      (5, 2): m("1", "10", "1")}), (5, 2))
+        self.assertEqual(select_rule({(3, 2): m("1", "9", "1.004"),
+                                      (5, 2): m("1", "9", "1.003")}), (5, 2))
+
+    def test_p09_existing_samples_seed42_and_reports_identical(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            directory = Path(directory)
+            for stem, options in (
+                ("sample_events", ()),
+                ("sample_events_v2", ("--recipe", ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models.json")),
+                ("sample_events_retest", ("--recipe", RECIPE)),
+                ("sample_events_equipment", ("--recipe", RECIPE, "--equipment", ROOT / "recipes/equipment.json")),
+            ):
+                result = cli(directory, "src.main", ROOT / "data" / (stem + ".csv"), *options)
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+            generated = directory / "gen_seed42.csv"
+            generate(42, 500, generated, ROOT / "recipes/generator.json")
+            for suffix in ("", ".meta.json"):
+                self.assertEqual(Path(str(generated) + suffix).read_bytes(),
+                                 (ROOT / "data/generated" / ("gen_seed42.csv" + suffix)).read_bytes())
+            result = cli(directory, "src.main", generated, "--recipe", RECIPE,
+                         "--equipment", ROOT / "recipes/equipment_generated.json", "--summary",
+                         "--trace", "GEN_0005", "--trace", "GEN_0030")
+            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+            reports = list((directory / "reports").iterdir())
+            self.assertEqual(len(reports), 20)
+            for report in reports:
+                with self.subTest(report=report.name):
+                    self.assertEqual(report.read_bytes(), (ROOT / "reports" / report.name).read_bytes())
+            print("P09: 20 reports + seed42 CSV/meta byte-identical", flush=True)
+
+    def test_p10_restart_csv_meta_and_reports_reproducible(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            directory = Path(directory)
+            for seed, products in ((42, 500), (7, 200)):
+                snapshots = []
+                for iteration in (1, 2):
+                    work = directory / (str(seed) + "-" + str(iteration))
+                    work.mkdir()
+                    output = work / "events.csv"
+                    generate(seed, products, output, CONFIG)
+                    result = cli(work, "src.main", output, "--recipe", RECIPE, "--equipment", EQUIPMENT,
+                                 "--summary", "--trace", "GEN_0001")
+                    self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+                    snapshots.append({p.relative_to(work).as_posix(): p.read_bytes() for p in work.rglob("*") if p.is_file()})
+                self.assertEqual(snapshots[0], snapshots[1])
+                print("P10: seed=%d products=%d CSV/meta/6 reports byte-identical" % (seed, products), flush=True)
+
+    def test_p11_t63_total_held_average_is_reported(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            result = cli(directory, "src.evaluate", "--config", ROOT / "recipes/generator.json",
+                         "--seeds", "0-29", "--rules", "3:2", "--out", "baseline", timeout=30)
+            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+            text = result.stdout.decode("utf-8")
+            self.assertIn("8.50", text)
+            self.assertIn("13.73", text, "T63 requires both total held mean 13.73 and normal held mean 8.50")
+
+    def test_p12_delay_rows_after_error_insertion(self):
+        config, _, recipe, book = load_setup(CONFIG)
+        generator = Generator(42, config, recipe.models, book)
+        normal = generator.normal_events(500)
+        rows, _ = generator.inject_errors(normal)
+        analysis = build_analysis(rows, recipe.models, book)
+        gaps = []
+        for resumed in analysis.restarts:
+            stop = analysis.stop_at(resumed.equipment, resumed.row_index)
+            intervening = resumed.row_index - stop.row_index - 1
+            gaps.append((resumed.equipment, stop.row_index, resumed.row_index, intervening))
+        self.assertTrue(gaps)
+        print("P12 final CSV stop/restart/intervening rows:", gaps, flush=True)
+        # The published definition says rows, with no exclusion for rejected injected rows.
+        self.assertTrue(all(gap[3] <= config["restart"]["delay_rows"] for gap in gaps),
+                        "A due restart must precede the next product row; error insertion delays it: %r" % gaps)
+
+    def test_p13_full_sized_restart_rejections_preserve_all_state(self):
+        config, _, recipe, book = load_setup(CONFIG)
+        for seed, products in ((42, 500), (7, 200)):
+            _, _, rows, counts = build_rows(seed, products, config, recipe.models, book)
+            expected = build_analysis(rows, recipe.models, book).decisions
+            process = RecipeProcess(recipe.models, book)
+            rejected = 0
+            for event, decision in zip(rows, expected):
+                before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment)) if not decision.accepted else None
+                actual = process.apply(event)
+                self.assertEqual(actual, decision)
+                if not actual.accepted:
+                    self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
+                    rejected += 1
+            self.assertGreater(rejected, 0)
+            self.assertEqual(rejected, sum(counts.values()))
+            print("P13: seed=%d products=%d all %d rows replayed, %d rejected full states unchanged" %
+                  (seed, products, len(rows), rejected), flush=True)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()
